### [1] [Finite Element-Based Material Learning via Automatic Differentiation: Learning constitutive neural network models from full-field deformation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05199)
*Matthias Knipper,Chenyi Ji,Malte Brand,Kevin Linka*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出FE-MAD框架，将本构神经网络嵌入JAX-FEM非线性求解器，通过自动微分实现端到端梯度优化，无需解析伴随或离线代理模型，在三个异构全场变形数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于均匀应力-应变实验的标定方法受限于高维可训练参数，而现有方法（有限元模型更新、弱形式方法、神经算子模型）在通用性、鲁棒性和计算效率之间存在权衡，急需一种兼具三者优势的框架。

Method: 将本构神经网络（灰箱CANN和白箱CANN）集成于JAX-FEM非线性求解器中，利用前向和反向自动微分计算牛顿切线刚度及损失梯度，实现端到端可微分的参数优化。

Result: 在三个开放实验数据集上验证：1) 带孔拉伸试样的全场DIC数据；2) 仅含一维拉伸轮廓和全局力-位移曲线的稀疏数据场景；3) 基体-夹杂两相系统的本构识别，并泛化至22个未见样本。

Conclusion: FE-MAD框架成功结合有限元方法的通用性、自动微分的计算效率与本构神经网络的表达能力，为异构全场变形数据下的材料本构模型参数标定提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The identification of constitutive neural network models from heterogeneous full-field deformation data provides a robust alternative to traditional calibration methods based on homogeneous stress-strain experiments, particularly given the high dimensionality of trainable parameters. Existing approaches must balance generality, robustness, and computational efficiency: Conventional finite element model updating is broadly applicable but computationally demanding; weak-form methods offer efficiency but are sensitive to noise and data scarcity; neural operator models are highly expressive but require extensive training datasets. This work presents FE-MAD (Finite Element-Based Material learning via Automatic Differentiation), an end-to-end differentiable framework that integrates a constitutive neural network model within a JAX-FEM nonlinear solver and identifies its parameters through gradient-based minimization of a measurement-mismatch loss. Newton tangent stiffness and loss gradients are computed automatically using forward- and reverse-mode automatic differentiation throughout the entire pipeline, thereby removing the need for analytic adjoints or offline surrogate models. FE-MAD is demonstrated for two architectures: a grey-box Constitutive Artificial Neural Network (CANN), a polyconvex, fully connected model with high flexibility, and a white-box CANN, an expert-system network with phenomenologically interpretable strain-energy terms. Focusing on incompressible isotropic hyperelasticity, FE-MAD is evaluated on three open experimental datasets: (1) full digital image correlation (DIC) of a perforated tensile specimen, (2) a reduced-data scenario with a one-dimensional stretch profile and global force-displacement curve, and (3) a heterogeneous matrix-inclusion system in which both phases constitutive laws are identified and generalized to twenty-two previously unseen samples.

</details>


### [2] [A differentiable machine learning small-angle X-ray scattering analysis framework for structure elucidation of lipid nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05200)
*Maria Bånkestad,Sandra Barman,Magnus Röding,Erik Kaunisto,Viktoriia Meklesh,Audrey Gallud,Marco Mendez,Marianna Yanez Arteta,Stefan Norberg,Ann Terry,Smita Chakraborty,Shun Yu,Jerk Rönnols,Sepideh Pashami*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种机器学习加速的可微框架，用于分析异质、多分散脂质纳米颗粒（LNP）的小角X射线散射（SAXS）数据。该框架结合了核壳颗粒模型、高斯随机场内部结构、神经代理模型以及可微的粒度分布积分层，将预测成本降低了四个数量级，并能够进行大规模多起点拟合和集成可辨识性分析。应用于MC3 LNP数据表明，相似的SAXS拟合结果可能源于不同的参数模式，且实验拟合由粒度分布与内部结构参数间的权衡主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAXS是LNP的重要表征技术，但从SAXS数据中恢复内部结构和尺寸分布是一个具有非唯一解的逆问题。现有的真实模型计算成本过高，难以进行系统探索。

Method: 提出一个机器学习加速的可微框架，包含：(1) 核壳颗粒模型结合高斯随机场内部结构；(2) 神经代理模型替代单分散SAXS映射；(3) 可微层用于粒度分布积分。利用可微性实现大规模多起点拟合和集成可辨识性分析。

Result: 代理模型将预测成本降低了四个数量级。应用于合成数据和实验MC3 LNP数据，发现近乎相同的SAXS拟合结果可能来自不同的参数模式，实验拟合主要由尺寸分布与内部结构参数之间的权衡主导。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了LNP的SAXS分析中逆问题的非唯一性，通过机器学习加速和可微优化提供了系统探索参数空间的能力，揭示了参数之间的耦合关系。

Abstract: Lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) are efficient delivery systems for negatively charged nucleic acids. Their multi-component architecture yields a core-shell structure. Small-angle X-ray scattering (SAXS) is an important characterization technique for LNPs, but recovering internal structure and size distribution from SAXS is an inverse problem with non-unique solutions. Realistic models are often too expensive for systematic exploration. We introduce a machine-learning-accelerated, differentiable framework for SAXS analysis of heterogeneous, polydisperse LNPs. The forward model combines a core-shell particle with a Gaussian random-field interior, a neural surrogate for the monodisperse SAXS map, and a differentiable layer integrating over particle-size distributions. The surrogate reduces prediction cost by four orders of magnitude, while differentiability enables large-scale multi-start fitting and ensemble identifiability analysis. Applied to synthetic and experimental MC3 LNP data, the framework shows that near-identical SAXS fits can arise from distinct parameter modes, with the experimental fits dominated by a trade-off between size-distribution and interior-structure parameters.

</details>


### [3] [Multi-Fidelity Learning with Shallow Recurrent Decoders for Reactor Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05202)
*Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,J. Nathan Kutz,Antonio Cammi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用浅层循环解码器（Shallow Recurrent Decoders）这一新型机器学习架构，将点动力学模型的低保真度时间序列数据映射到扩散方程的高保真度全状态解，以低计算成本实现反应堆物理中的多保真度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反应堆物理中，高保真度中子学建模计算成本高昂，导致高保真度数据稀缺；而低保真度模型（如点动力学）虽可高效生成大量数据，但精度不足。因此需要一种方法将低保真度模型映射到高保真度模型，以平衡计算效率与精度。

Method: 采用浅层循环解码器（Shallow Recurrent Decoders）架构，该架构原设计用于将局部或全局测量值的时间轨迹映射到高维状态空间。本文将其扩展应用于以集总模型（点动力学模型）的解作为输入，映射到多群扩散方程的全状态解。

Result: 在基准反应堆几何上，该方法能够在多种输入条件下，以极低的计算成本将点动力学模型成功映射到扩散解，验证了多保真度信息融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 浅层循环解码器能够有效地将低保真度集总模型的时间序列数据映射至高保真度空间分布解，为反应堆物理中高效的多保真度建模提供了新的可行途径，大幅降低了计算开销。

Abstract: In reactor physics, neutronics can be treated with different fidelity levels, according to the needs of the user. On one hand, the precise modeling of neutrons' behaviour in reactor physics is often expensive and time-consuming due to the high computational costs to numerically solve the Boltzmann transport equation. Conversely, by adopting suitable assumptions, such as the SP$_N$, diffusion theory, and point kinetics, it is possible to generate efficiently low-fidelity data. From the perspective of surrogate models, this computational limitation translates into a scarcity of high-fidelity data and a significant amount of low-fidelity data. Given this difference in fidelity levels, it would be interesting to develop a suitable procedure to map low-fidelity models towards higher fidelity models; for instance, one could obtain the solution to a multi-group diffusion equation starting from time-series data obtained from a point kinetics model. Indeed, this work investigates this possibility by leveraging multi-fidelity information with Shallow Recurrent Decoders, a novel machine learning architecture able to map time-series observations to the full state of the reactor. This technique has been designed to use local or global measurements as input and map their temporal trajectories to the high-dimensional state; by the same logic, in principle, this architecture can also be used when the input is formed by the solution of a lumped model. This work applies this idea to a benchmark reactor geometry, mapping the point kinetics model to the diffusion solution under various input conditions, with much less computational costs.

</details>


### [4] [Newton's Identity in Finite-Bead Fermionic Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05442)
*A. Chaudhary,J. Valenzuela*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对谐波陷阱中非相互作用费米子，从路径积分的压缩行列式形式推导出有限虚时间切片（珠子）下的精确递推关系，并利用牛顿恒等式与分拆理论得到一维n费米子有限珠子配分函数的闭式表达式，进而解析计算了任意参数下的热力学能量、哈密顿能量和比热。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续极限递推关系不适用于离散时间配分函数，需建立适用于任意有限珠子数和短时传播子选择的精确递推方法。

Method: 从路径积分的压缩行列式形式出发，推导有限珠子递推关系；建立该递推与牛顿恒等式的对应关系；运用分拆理论中的闭式结果得到一维n费米子有限珠子配分函数的精确表达式。

Result: 获得了任意粒子数n、珠子数N、虚时间步长τ及传播子选择下一维费米子配分函数的精确解析形式，并由此解析计算了热力学能量、哈密顿能量和比热。

Conclusion: 该有限珠子递推关系为离散时间路径积分中的费米子问题提供了精确解析框架，弥补了连续极限方法在有限珠子情形下的空白。

Abstract: For non-interacting fermions in a harmonic trap, the partition function at any discrete number of imaginary time slices (or beads) and for any choice of short-time propagator admits an exact recursion relation derived directly from the contracted determinant form of the path integral. This finite-bead recursion is distinct from earlier continuum-limit recursions, which do not apply to the discrete time partition functions. By identifying a direct correspondence between this recursion and Newton's identity, application of a closed-form result from the theory of partitions provides an exact expression for the one-dimensional $n$-fermion finite-bead partition function. From this, the Thermodynamic and Hamiltonian energies and specific heats are analytically calculated for any $n$, $N$, $τ$, and propagator choice.

</details>


### [5] [Towards stable and accurate electron dynamics via neural network based time-dependent variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05850)
*Weizhong Fu,Zhe Li,Yubing Qian,Ruichen Li,Weiluo Ren,Ji Chen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出神经基矢含时变分蒙特卡洛框架（NB-tVMC），通过将时间演化约束在由神经基矢张成的紧致自定义流形上，实现了稳定、高精度的电子实时动力学模拟，在氢原子、拉伸氢分子等体系中达到基准级精度，并准确提取了氦和铍原子的动态极化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络变分蒙特卡洛在静态计算中取得了前所未有的精度，但其向实时演化的扩展面临稳定性挑战，亟需一种能够实现长期稳定演化的方法。

Method: 将时间演化约束在由神经基矢张成的紧致自定义流形上，从而绕过不稳定性问题，实现长期稳定演化。

Result: 在激光驱动的氢原子和拉伸氢分子的偶极响应模拟中达到基准级精度，并准确提取了氦和铍原子的动态极化率。

Conclusion: 神经网络波函数在精确描述实时电子动力学方面具有巨大潜力，为复杂含时电子现象的第一性原理模拟开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Real-time dynamics of interacting electrons lies at the interface between quantum mechanics and non-equilibrium physics, governing the microscopic origin of ultrafast phenomena of molecules and nano-materials. Though neural network variational Monte Carlo has achieved unprecedented accuracy for stationary state calculations, its extension to real-time evolution remains challenging. In this work, we introduce the neural basis time-dependent variational Monte Carlo framework, which achieves stable and highly accurate simulations of electron dynamics. By constraining the time evolution to a compact, customized manifold spanned by the neural basis, we effectively bypass instability issues and achieve long-term stable evolution. Moreover, we demonstrate that this framework yields benchmark-quality accuracy in simulating the laser-driven dipole responses of the hydrogen atom and a stretched hydrogen molecule, and accurately extracts the dynamic polarizabilities of helium and beryllium atoms. Our work reveals the vast potential of neural network wavefunctions for accurately describing real-time electron dynamics and establishes a promising new route for first-principles simulations of complex, time-dependent electronic phenomena.

</details>

### [6] [Quantum Circuit Complexity as a Measure of Particle Creation in Bouncing Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05235)
*Samak Boonpan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量方法，在非奇异性弹跳宇宙背景中精确研究了量子标量场的电路复杂度演化。发现复杂度在弹跳点保持有限，由量子态的压缩主导，且弹跳后复杂度的增长与宇宙学粒子产生强相关，其中"啁啾"贡献作为几何记忆量化了粒子产生的信息成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用微扰方法处理弹跳宇宙背景下的量子场复杂度，缺乏精确的非微扰框架来理解复杂度在弹跳过程中的演化行为及其与粒子产生的关系。

Method: 采用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量方法，建立非微扰精确框架，研究非奇异性弹跳宇宙背景中标量场的电路复杂度演化。

Result: 1) 复杂度在弹跳点保持有限；2) 复杂度由时空收缩导致的量子态压缩主导；3) 弹跳后复杂度增长与宇宙学粒子产生强相关；4) "啁啾"贡献作为几何记忆，量化了粒子产生的信息成本。

Conclusion: 非微扰框架揭示了复杂度演化与粒子产生之间的内在联系，指出复杂度中的"啁啾"贡献可作为非平凡时空几何中粒子产生信息成本的量化指标。

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of circuit complexity for a quantum scalar field in a non-singular bouncing cosmological background. Unlike previous perturbative approaches, we utilize the Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant method to derive an exact non-perturbative framework for the complexity evolution across the bounce. We demonstrate that the complexity remains finite at the bounce, dominated by the squeezing of the quantum state due to spacetime contraction. Crucially, we find that the post-bounce growth of complexity exhibits a strong correlation with cosmological particle production. Our analysis reveals that the "chirping" contribution to complexity acts as a geometric memory of the transition, effectively quantifying the information cost of particle creation in non-trivial spacetime geometries.

</details>


### [7] [Equation of State Parameters for Fluid of Stringy Extended Objects in Cosmology with Cosmological Constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05243)
*Soon-Tae Hong,Bum-Hoon Lee*

TL;DR: 本文在高维宇宙学（HDC）中构建了弦论扩展体（包括有质量和无质量）的强能量条件（SECs），推导出状态方程参数 $w \geq -(D-4)/D$（$D \geq 5$），并揭示了该条件在辐射主导和物质主导时期均适用。还研究了与四维Hawking-Penrose极限的关系，以及弱能量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维宇宙学背景下，研究弦论扩展体的能量条件（特别是强能量条件SEC），以理解其状态方程参数约束，并建立与四维点粒子极限之间的联系。

Method: 构建有质量和无质量弦论扩展体在高维宇宙学（含宇宙学常数Λ）中的强能量条件，推导EoS参数约束；分析HDC中EoS参数与四维Hawking-Penrose极限下点粒子EoS参数的关系；将EoS参数分解为点粒子贡献、宇宙学常数贡献和扩展体自由度贡献；同时分析弱能量条件。

Result: 有质量和无质量弦论扩展体在D维（D≥5）宇宙学中的EoS参数满足 $w \geq -(D-4)/D$；该约束在辐射主导和物质主导时期均成立；揭示了HDC中EoS参数与四维Hawking-Penrose极限的关系；获得了EoS参数的点粒子、宇宙学常数和扩展体自由度三部分贡献表达式。

Conclusion: 弦论SEC对EoS参数施加了一个普适约束，该约束在辐射和物质主导时期均有效，且与维度D相关，为高维弦论宇宙学提供了重要理论限制。

Abstract: We construct the strong energy conditions (SECs) for both massive and massless stringy extended objects in the higher dimensional cosmology (HDC) with cosmological constant $Λ$. Exploiting these conditions, we find the equation of state (EoS) parameters \mbox{$w\geq -(D-4)/D$} for both the massive and massless stringy extended objects in $D$ {$(D\geq 5)$} dimensional cosmology. The stringy SECs impose a universal constraint on $w$ that remains valid across both radiation- and matter-dominated eras. We elucidate the relations between the EoS parameter in the HDC with cosmological constant and that of Hawking--Penrose limit for the massive and massless point particles in the four dimensions. We evaluate the EoS parameters in terms of the contributions from the point particle property, cosmological constant, and extended object degrees of freedom, respectively. We also investigate the weak energy condition for the massive and massless stringy extended objects in the HDC, and those for the massive and massless point particles in the four dimensions,~respectively.

</details>

*Joan Solà Peracaula*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出运行真空模型（RVM）作为替代ΛCDM的理论框架，其中真空能量密度ρ_vac随哈勃率H及其时间导数演化，而非固定常数。RVM通过量子场论在弯曲时空中的效应，统一解释了早期宇宙暴涨（无需暴胀子场）和晚期暗能量，同时牛顿引力常数G也随H对数缓慢演化。该模型与DESI等观测数据吻合，支持动态暗能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ΛCDM模型中刚性的宇宙学常数Λ与宇宙膨胀历史存在长期危机，而随宇宙膨胀演化的Λ在直觉上更为合理。作者希望基于弯曲时空量子场论，构建一个统一的量子场论框架，从基本原理出发描述整个宇宙，同时解释暗能量和暴涨的起源。

Method: 基于运行真空模型（RVM）框架，利用弯曲时空量子场论（QFT）计算量子效应对真空能量密度（VED）的贡献，使其成为H及其时间导数的函数：ρ_vac = ρ_vac(H, Ḣ, Ḧ, ...)。在晚期宇宙中主导项为~m_Pl²H²（缓慢演化），早期宇宙中高阶项~H⁴驱动快速暴涨。牛顿常数G同样作为ln H的函数对数演化。

Result: RVM实现了无需暴胀子场的暴涨机制（RVM-暴涨），区别于Starobinsky暴涨（其中H非恒定）。动态VED自然符合DESI等当前观测对动态暗能量的偏好，优于刚性Λ项。同时，G的缓慢对数演化对自然基本常数可能随宇宙膨胀轻微变化的讨论具有重要意义。

Conclusion: RVM提供了暗能量与暴涨的统一量子场论框架，其中动态真空能量密度是理论预测而非人为引入，与观测数据吻合，可作为描述宇宙整体演化的现实理论基础。

Abstract: The concordance $Λ$CDM model, based on a rigid $Λ$-term for the entire cosmic history, has been in crisis for a long time. In our expanding Universe, an evolving $Λ$ with the expansion is intuitively much more reasonable. In the running vacuum model (RVM) framework, based on quantum field theory (QFT) in curved spacetime, quantum effects induce a vacuum energy density (VED) $ρ_{\rm vac}=Λ/(8πG)$ which is a function of the Hubble rate $H$ and its time derivatives, $ρ_{\rm vac}=ρ_{\rm vac}(H, \dot{H},\ddot{H},\dots)$. Currently, $ρ_{\rm vac}$ evolves very slowly with the expansion, $δρ_{\rm vac}\sim {\cal O}(m_{Pl} ^2 H^2)$, and this fact provides a possible fundamental origin of dark energy (DE), conceived as dynamical vacuum energy. In the RVM, Newton's $G$ is also evolving, but much more slowly (logarithmically with $H$): $G=G(\ln H)$. In the very early universe, the vacuum fluctuations induce higher (even) powers, e.g. $\sim H^4$, capable of triggering fast inflation in a very short period, in which $H$ is very large and approximately constant. This is the mechanism of `RVM-inflation'. It does not require an `inflaton' field since inflation is brought about by pure QFT effects on the dynamical background. It differs from Starobinsky's inflation, where $H$ is never constant. Furthermore, the dynamics of $ρ_{\rm vac}(H)$ and $G(H)$ can also have implications on the frequently discussed possibility that the fundamental `constants' of Nature can be mildly evolving with the cosmic expansion. Putting things together, a unified QFT framework of dark energy and inflation ensues as a realistic theory for the description of the universe as a whole on fundamental grounds. In it, dynamical VED is predicted and is much welcomed, since it fits in with current DESI measurements, preferring dynamical DE over a rigid $Λ$ term.

</details>


### [9] [Spherically symmetric, asymptotically flat Berwald vacuum solutions in Finsler gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05427)

Abstract: So-called Berwald-Finsler spacetimes are Finsler spacetimes that are closest to pseudo-Riemannian geometry, as their canonical nonlinear connection defines an affine connection on spacetime. In spherical symmetry, these geometries can be used to describe the gravitational field outside of compact objects. We solve the Finsler gravity vacuum equation for $SO(3)$-symmetric Berwald spacetimes that are asyptotically flat, but not Ricci flat. We find that among all spherically symmetric Berwald spacetimes, only one class is compatible with asymptotic flatness and a well defined causal structure. For this class, we completely solve the Finsler gravity vacuum equation and find three families of non-Ricci flat solutions -- which represent the first non-trivial, exact spherically symmetric vacuum solutions. They are so-called $(α,β)$-Finsler spacetimes that are constructed from a pseudo-Riemannnian metric and a 1-form. In particular, we show, by providing a concrete example, that in Finsler geometry there exist $SO(3)$-symmetric, asymptotically flat vacuum solutions that are not Ricci flat; these solutions are promising candidates to model the gravitational field around compact objects, beyond their Riemannian description.

</details>

Abstract: We study the curvature conditions introduced in [Class. Quant. Grav. 27, 152002] to predict focal points for trapped spacelike submanifolds in spacetimes of arbitrary dimensions, with the purpose of generalizing Penrose's singularity theorem to compact trapped submanifolds (CTMs) of codimension higher than two. Abstract: We construct a new class of black-bounce solutions sourced by a self-interacting 3-form field minimally coupled to general relativity and a scalar field. The 3-form field, which naturally arises in string theory, supergravity, and cosmological models, provides the anisotropic effective stresses required to sustain regular geometries that interpolate smoothly between black holes and traversable wormholes. By exploiting the Hodge duality between a 3-form and a 1-form in four dimensions, we reduce the field equations and obtain exact solutions through the direct integration of the coupled equations of motion. In particular, the solutions are derived from algebraic combinations and manipulations of the Einstein, scalar, and 3-form field equations, starting from a complete action principle, without employing the usual reconstruction procedure in which the metric ansatz is imposed a priori and the matter sector is reconstructed afterwards. This approach reveals two distinct classes of solutions. The first one yields a globally phantom scalar field and a metric function with a characteristic arctangent dependence, reducing to the Schwarzschild-(anti) de Sitter spacetime in the limit of vanishing 3-form coupling. The second class produces a constant 3-form Lagrangian and, remarkably, a partially canonical scalar field, namely phantom only near the bounce and canonical outside the event horizon, a feature previously attainable mainly in modified theories of gravity, but which emerges here within pure general relativity. Both families are globally regular, as confirmed by the finiteness of the Kretschmann scalar, and exhibit an asymmetric horizon structure inherited from the 3-form energy-density distribution. These results demonstrate that the 3-form black-bounce framework is both mathematically consistent and observationally viable.

</details>

Using the new analytic results, we verify the numerical findings reported in early work and uncover multiple cases missed in the previous numerical analysis. These include: (i) an unbounded charge-mass ratio when the non-minimal coupling parameter $ξ$ is larger than $2κ$, (ii) the emergence of a traversable wormhole configuration for overcharged solutions with $ξ<0$, (iii) the non-monotonic turning behavior of the Hawking temperature as a function of the charge-mass ratio, and (iv) the presence of two divergent points in the constant-$Y$ heat capacity.

Abstract: A decade after the first direct detection of gravitational waves, the growing catalog of over one hundred confirmed events is revealing new insights into the spins of stellar-mass black holes. Spin measurements have long been heralded as a promising tracer of compact-object binary formation and evolution, as different formation channels predict unique spin signatures on a population level. In this review, we summarize the astrophysics, phenomenology, and current measurements of black hole spins. We begin with an overview of the predictions for black hole spin magnitudes and orientations from leading formation channels--isolated binary evolution, dynamical formation in clusters, formation in AGN disks, and hierarchical triples. We then describe the imprint of spin effects on the gravitational waveform and the measurability of spin in individual events. Finally, we review current population-level constraints on spin magnitudes, orientations, and effective spin parameters, including correlations with mass and redshift, and discuss their astrophysical implications. We conclude by highlighting open questions and future prospects, emphasizing how improved detector sensitivity will enable increasingly precise spin measurements for both individual events and the binary black hole population as a whole.

Abstract: Cosmological correlators receive contributions from both field interactions in the bulk of quasi-de Sitter (dS) spacetime and boundary terms at the end of inflation. While most of the research efforts focus on the former, boundary contributions are normally believed to be negligible or related to field redefinitions that are associated with redundancies of the bulk Lagrangian. In this paper, we revisit this topic in the light of the cosmological bootstrap. We first establish the correspondence between boundary terms and field redefinitions for cosmological correlators. This result provides a set of criteria for determining when boundary terms lead to non-vanishing contributions to cosmological observables. Next, we apply this general understanding to concrete examples of correlators from massive-exchange diagrams, in both dS-invariant and boost-breaking scenarios. For theories with dS isometries, both IBP and field redefinitions are used to relate derivative and non-derivative exchange diagrams, from which the leading boundary contributions and IR-divergent pieces are extracted; we also derive recursion relations among higher-derivative exchange correlators. For theories with broken dS boosts, we use IBP and EoM to reduce exchange diagrams to a basis of independent templates, and then present a classification of the boundary contributions in the general effective field theory framework. These results pave the way for a more systematic investigation on the boundary physics of the inflationary spacetime.

Abstract: GW190521 and GW231123 have been reported as short-duration gravitational-wave transients consistent with very massive binary black hole (BBH) coalescences whose inferred parameters, i.e., exceptionally high total masses and spin magnitudes, challenge standard isolated binary stellar evolution. We test a topological dark matter (TDM) interpretation invoking cosmic domain walls by fitting a physically motivated domain wall template to the LIGO Hanford and Livingston strain data. The BBH hypothesis is individually favored, with $\log_{10}\mathcal{B}_{\rm BBH/TDM}=12.2$ and $11.3$ for GW231123 and GW190521, respectively. However, these values are lower than those typically recovered from matched maximum a posteriori BBH waveforms injected into nearby noise segments. We further perform, for the first time, a joint fit in which domain wall signals from a single underlying scalar field are constrained simultaneously by both events. Although not favored over BBH signals, we find the two events are consistent with a common scalar field, with shared TDM parameters agreeing across independent noise realizations and sky locations. We further find that injected TDM transients are systematically recovered under the BBH hypothesis with large spin parameters, revealing a morphological degeneracy that could mask genuine domain wall signals. This analysis demonstrates that multi-event parameter consistency tests provide a new discriminant for domain wall dark matter searches in upcoming observing runs.

Abstract: We derive equations of motion for scalar particles self-consistently interacting with a scalar field,including the radiation produced by the particles' acceleration. Our approach differs in three key aspects from current methods: (1) we assume a small but finite discretization length scale $h$, which allows us to treat the particle as a small but finite object, (2) we choose the state vector for the system before deriving equations of motion, and (3) we formulate the equations explicitly in the lab frame and not in a manifestly covariant manner. This approach, which is self-consistent, happens to greatly simplify the resulting equations and their derivation, and is directly suitable for numerical calculations. The result is an effective source method which generalizes to electrodynamics or general relativity in a straightforward manner (although we do not consider this here). We then provide two possible discretizations of these equations, based on finite volumes and spectral methods, and show results of one-dimensional calculations. These calculations show excellent agreement with analytic solutions.

A rigorous mathematical examination of this transformation reveals a violation of the multivariable chain rule. The spatial derivatives do not vanish in the physical reference frame. The mathematical framework presented by the authors remains identical to the well-established semiclassical Van Vleck propagator, which is exact exclusively for quadratic potentials.

Abstract: The quantum Rabi model captures fundamental aspects of light--matter interaction, where symmetry dictates both spectra and dynamics. In the deep-strong-coupling regime, we show that anisotropy reconstructs the spectrum and turns complete collapse-revival dynamics into incomplete revivals even near degeneracy. With adiabatic state preparation and joint tomography, we resolve an anisotropy-induced ground-state parity switch, a crossing that has no analogue in the isotropic model. We further observe selective tunnelling associated with hidden symmetry in biased Rabi models and track its anisotropic displacement within the same device. These results establish a controllable route to engineering nonperturbative light--matter Hamiltonians, where symmetry, spectrum, and dynamics can be programmed independently.

Abstract: Determining the minimal distance between a target channel and a convex hull of predefined set of implementable channels is a fundamental problem in quantum resource theory, and provides key guidance for experimental implementations. Abstract: We present a symmetry-adapted qubit encoding with complete active space (SAE-CAS) for quantum chemistry on fault-tolerant and near-term quantum processors. Building on exact-symmetry encodings, we extend symmetry-adapted mappings to approximate $Z$-symmetries corresponding to frozen-core and virtual orbitals, thereby reducing qubit requirements without significant loss of accuracy. The same framework also accommodates the Bravyi--Kitaev mapping, yielding an SAE-CAS-BK variant that is unitarily equivalent to SAE-CAS. We provide an open-source implementation in the Python package QuantumSymmetry. SAE-CAS offers a route to resource-efficient molecular simulations on fault-tolerant and near-term quantum processors.

Abstract: Uncertainty principle, one of the most iconic features of quantum mechanics, was originally viewed as a fundamental limitation. Since the inception of quantum information science, researchers began to use it to achieve quantum advantages. To better understand the origin of these advantages, an essential question is: To what extent can the uncertainty principle's signatures be simulated by a single measurement? As a single measurement clearly cannot demonstrate the uncertainty principle, such a simulation, if exists, implies the claimed advantages may either stem from other quantum features, or just be reproducible in a less resourceful way. In this work, we report a series of noise-robust no-go theorems, showing that strong enough signatures of uncertainty principle cannot be simulated by a single measurement, even when assisted by quantum pre- or post-processing. This signature is modelled by complementary instruments. We completely characterise complementary instruments by a numerically feasible measure and show that they are necessary and sufficient resources for the advantage in an operational task that aims to unambiguously send classical information.

Abstract: The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in diamond has attracted considerable attention as a highly sensitive quantum sensor that can operate at room temperature. Abstract: We propose quantum algorithms for preparing thermal states via the simulation of the thermofield double states. The key idea is to leverage double-bracket quantum algorithms to implement imaginary-time evolution on thermofield double states, whose reduced state realizes the Gibbs state. Our method, termed double-bracket thermofield double (DB-TFD), introduces two variants. The first, the vanilla DB-TFD algorithm, directly implements imaginary-time evolution using double-bracket quantum imaginary-time evolution. The second, poly DB-TFD, employs double-bracket quantum signal processing to approximate the imaginary-time evolution operator via a polynomial transformation. We demonstrate that the complexity of the poly DB-TFD algorithm scales exponentially with the inverse temperature in a broad practical regime. This scaling is consistent with existing methods, and numerical simulations support the corresponding theoretical bound. We further demonstrate the utility of DB-TFD in quantum Boltzmann machines for generative modeling, achieving improved performance compared with variational imaginary-time evolution approaches. These results establish DB-TFD as a promising route for thermal state preparation in the near-term and early-fault-tolerant regimes.

Abstract: Fixed-ansatz Variational Quantum Eigensolvers (VQE), such as the Unitary Coupled Cluster with Singles and Doubles (UCCSD) framework, frequently suffer from severe initialization paralyzation and zero-gradient traps when evaluated using the non-local Bravyi-Kitaev (BK) fermion-to-qubit mapping. In this work, we systematically demonstrate how the Adaptive Derivative-Assembled Pseudo-Trotter (ADAPT-VQE) framework shatters these structural limitations across three distinct electronic and geometric molecular pillars: Lithium Hydride ($\text{LiH}$), Hydrogen Fluoride ($\text{HF}$), and Water ($\text{H}_2\text{O}$), under heavily stretched or asymmetric multi-reference configurations. To bypass the severe $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ computational bottlenecks of dense matrix exponentiation and Singular Value Decomposition on larger registers, we implement a highly optimized, vector-based Taylor series expansion state-evolution engine. Our numerical results show that the accelerated ADAPT-VQE framework achieves instant, exact Full Configuration Interaction (FCI) convergence within the very first macro-cycle across all three molecular systems, maintaining absolute numerical stability up to a 12-qubit register space. This study establishes a robust, hardware-efficient path for simulating strongly correlated, highly polarized triatomic chemical environments on near-term local architectures.

Abstract: Controlled time-evolution circuits select forward or backward Hamiltonian time evolution according to the state of an ancilla qubit. We develop an efficient recursive algorithm that, for an arbitrary Pauli-sum Hamiltonian, partitions the input Pauli terms into groups and assigns to each group a sign-flip Pauli string that anti-commutes with the in-group terms, thereby removing ancilla control from the grouped time-evolution blocks. Numerical benchmarks on random Hamiltonians and structured spin Hamiltonians show reductions in compiled T depth and compiled CX depth. For a Kagome Hamiltonian with 24 spins under full connectivity, the proposed construction reduces the compiled T depth by 85.2% and the compiled CX depth by 68.9%, compared with a conventional implementation that decomposes the Hamiltonian into individual Pauli terms and implements the controlled time evolution of each term by directly adding the ancilla qubit to the corresponding Pauli-rotation circuit.

Here, we challenge this perspective, showing that a more refined analysis of the asymptotic performance of work extraction reveals significant differences in the performance for the two different classes of free operations. Precisely, we focus on the trade-off between the extraction rate and its reliability, characterized by the optimal asymptotic speed at which the extraction error can be suppressed. We establish that the reliability of Gibbs-preserving operations and of thermal operations are respectively characterized by the Petz and the sandwiched Rényi relative entropies, demonstrating that the former in general strictly outperforms the latter, and providing new interpretations of several information-theoretic divergences. Our analysis reveals that operational constraints such as energy conservation impose stronger limitations on the achievable precision of quantum tasks than can be inferred from their asymptotic rates, thereby questioning the use of Gibbs-preserving operations as a mathematically convenient substitute for the physically realizable thermal processes.

Abstract: Semiconductor nanostructures are central to many developing technologies. *Edwin Tham,Michael L. Goldman,Shantanu Debnath,Ashay N. Patel,Jyothi Saraladevi,Jason Nguyen,Erik Nielsen,Neal Pisenti,Kenneth Wright,John Gamble,Nicolas Delfosse*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文在离子阱量子计算机上无需硬件重配置，演示了9种不同量子纠错码（包括qLDPC码、拓扑码和级联码），使用编码4个逻辑量子比特到18个物理量子比特的qLDPC码，逻辑错误率比此前超导实现低9倍，并达到盈亏平衡性能。采用新型OMG架构实现可寻址的中间电路测量与重置，无需离子传输或专用冷却离子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速率量子LDPC码是实现容错量子计算的重要候选方案，但其硬件实现面临长程耦合等挑战。需要探索如何在现有硬件平台上高效实现这些编码，并验证其性能优势。

Method: 利用离子阱量子计算机的灵活性，在同一设备上无需硬件重配置即可演示三种不同家族（qLDPC码、拓扑码、级联码）共9种量子纠错码。采用新型OMG（光学-亚稳态-基态）架构实现可寻址的中间电路测量与重置，避免离子传输和专用冷却离子的需求。

Result: 使用编码4逻辑量子比特到18物理量子比特的qLDPC码，逻辑错误率比此前超导固态量子比特类似编码的演示低9倍。实现盈亏平衡性能，部分实例的量子比特寿命达到或略超过离子阱量子比特的寿命。

Conclusion: 离子阱平台凭借其灵活的量子比特连接性，可在无需硬件重配置的情况下支持多种不同拓扑结构的量子纠错码。新型OMG架构有效降低了实验中离子传输和冷却离子所需的资源开销。这展示了离子阱量子计算机实现高速率qLDPC码的可行性和优势。

Abstract: High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes are a leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computing. They feature higher encoding rates than planar alternatives such as the surface code, but their implementation often entails significant hardware hurdles like the need for long-range couplers. We leverage the flexibility of a trapped-ion quantum computer to demonstrate nine quantum error-correcting codes with starkly different qubit connectivity requirements on a single device without any hardware reconfiguration. These experiments span three families of quantum error-correcting codes: qLDPC codes, topological codes, and concatenated codes. With a qLDPC code encoding 4 logical qubits into 18 physical qubits, we achieve a logical error rate up to $9\times$ better than a previous demonstration of a similar code on superconducting solid-state qubits. Moreover, our implementation exhibits breakeven performance, with some instances achieving qubit lifetimes comparable to or slightly exceeding that of our trapped-ion qubits. We use a novel implementation of the optical-metastable-ground (OMG) architecture for addressable mid-circuit measurement and reset, which enables us to perform these experiments without any ion transport or dedicated coolant ions, requirements that typically consume a large fraction of the runtime or ion count of trapped-ion quantum computers.

</details>


### [72] [Quantum element-wise transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06456)
*Zane M. Rossi,Rahul Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子数值线性代数中一些现有算法效率低下或实现不明确的矩阵变换（尤其是元素级多项式函数变换），提出了改进的量子算法，将空间复杂度在函数度数上实现指数级降低，并修正了先前构造中的错误，同时展示了在机器学习、模拟和信号处理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子奇异值变换（QSVT）和线性组合酉算子（LCU）等技术能够很好地处理许多基本的数值线性代数任务（如对嵌入酉过程的矩阵谱施加多项式函数、对块编码进行线性组合等），但存在一些有用的矩阵变换（如元素级多项式函数变换）无法被现有量子算法高效实现，或者实现方式不明确。因此需要构造改进的量子算法来解决这些问题。

Method: 构造了改进的量子算法来实现元素级矩阵变换，相比先前工作，将所需空间复杂度在函数度数上实现了指数级降低，同时发现并纠正了先前构造中的错误。

Result: 实现了量子元素级变换的空间复杂度在函数度数上的指数级缩减，修正了先前工作中的错误。

Conclusion: 改进的量子算法可应用于机器学习、模拟和信号处理等领域。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for basic numerical linear algebraic tasks have proven essential for translating diverse problems to a unified quantum computational context. Many of these tasks -- e.g., applying a polynomial function to the spectrum of a matrix embedded in a unitary process (a so-called block encoding), or taking linear combinations of block encodings -- are well-addressed by techniques like quantum singular value transformation (QSVT) or linear combination of unitaries (LCU). However, there exist useful matrix transforms whose realization by existing quantum algorithms is unclear or inefficient. In this work we construct improved quantum algorithms for some of these transforms, the simplest of which is a polynomial function applied element-wise. We show the space required to compute quantum element-wise transforms can be reduced exponentially in the degree of the applied function compared to prior work, and raise and rectify errors in previous constructions. We present our algorithms alongside applications to machine learning, simulation, and signal processing.

</details>

### [73] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套体视学理论来量化LLM基准测试的覆盖范围，证明了可见豪斯多夫距离的上下界，经验发现三个独立排行榜的有效维度在2.86-4.80之间，结构盲点比亚军分数差距大两个数量级。同时解决了Gardner问题1.5（1995），建立了C²支撑函数的最优极小极大速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试的覆盖范围缺乏严格的理论分析，现有排行榜可能存在结构性盲点，即某些能力维度未被充分评估，导致排名不可靠。需要量化基准套件的能力覆盖完备性及其不确定性边界。

Method: 1) 体视学理论框架：用凸能力轮廓和可见豪斯多夫距离量化基准覆盖；2) 经验分析：对三个独立排行榜计算有效维度d_eff；3) 卡方投影模型：模拟不同先验下的排名翻转率；4) 子模贪心算法：寻找基准套件的稳定核心子集；5) 反事实验证：用特征结构预测基准可替换性；6) 最优恢复理论：解决Gardner问题1.5。

Result: 1) 三个排行榜有效维度d_eff ∈ [2.86, 4.80]；2) 结构盲点比亚军分数差距大两个数量级，比统计噪声大52-127倍；3) 半分割排名翻转率在0.38-0.49之间，500次随机分割中92%导致第一名交换；4) 4个基准构成稳定核心，7/12达到90%覆盖率，跨时间迁移保留率93-97%；5) 特征结构预测基准不可替代性（ρ=-0.69, p=0.013）和外部评估信息量（ρ=+0.38）；6) Gardner问题1.5的极小极大速率为Θ(R/(κ m^(2/(D-1))))。

Conclusion: LLM基准套件存在显著的结构盲点，当前排行榜排名不可靠性很高。基准覆盖理论可指导构建更高效的评估套件。Gardner问题的最优解为凸几何中的支撑函数恢复问题提供了理论极限。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [74] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Errorquake-10k基准，在10,000条查询上对21个开源LLM的回复进行0-4连续严重性评分，发现即使准确率匹配，不同模型的错误严重性分布存在显著差异。通过古登堡-里克特斜率b量化严重性分布，证明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余，提出严重性分布应作为准确率的补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准将所有错误视为等价，仅报告单一错误率，忽视了不同严重程度错误（如错误日期vs虚构法庭裁决）的数量级差异。作者旨在揭示准确率匹配下模型间错误严重性分布的系统性差异，并证明严重性分布承载错误率无法捕获的判别信息。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k基准（10,000查询，8领域，5难度层级，0-4连续严重性评分）；对21个开源模型拟合严重性分布指数b（古登堡-里克特上尾斜率）及95%自助法置信区间；进行519项三人类验证研究（ICC(2,k=3)=0.85）及LLM裁判排名验证（rho=0.89）；证明非冗余性定理（I(b; model | epsilon)=1.56 bits）；建立严重性机制分类学（kappa=0.83）分析错误类型随严重性的类别变化。

Result: 210个模型对中85对在匹配准确率（|Delta epsilon|<0.05）下具有不相交的95% b置信区间；密集模型规模与人类数据严重性呈显著负相关（rho_s=-0.86）；低严重性错误以检索错误为主（71%），高严重性错误以虚构为主（39%），且此构成随模型规模显著变化（p<0.0001）；64.5%的跨模型b方差无法被错误率解释。

Conclusion: 严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余，应在准确率之外作为独立指标报告。错误类型的严重性层级转移（检索→虚构）揭示了模型规模对错误模式的系统性影响，为模型评估提供了更细粒度的维度。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [75] [On the training of physics-informed neural operators for solving parametric partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06164)
*Nanxi Chen,Chuanjie Cui,Airong Chen,Sifan Wang,Rujin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了物理信息神经算子（PINO）的训练流程，涵盖架构设计、优化器选择、损失平衡和配点采样策略。实验表明CViT架构表现稳健，且PINN训练中的优化病理（梯度冲突、因果违反）同样存在于PINO中，相应的缓解算法也依然有效。精心设计的物理信息训练可在某些场景下匹敌甚至超越纯数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINO的稳健高效训练方法尚不明确，缺乏系统性理解，本文旨在填补这一空白，探究其关键训练组件并揭示优化挑战。

Method: 对比三种算子骨干网络（DeepONet、FNO、CViT）在五个参数化PDE系统上的表现，分析架构、优化器、损失平衡、配点采样策略的影响，并验证PINN优化病理在PINO中的存在性及缓解算法的有效性。

Result: CViT在所有基准测试中表现一致且稳健；PINN中的梯度冲突和因果违反等优化病理在PINO中自然出现；PINN的缓解算法在PINO中依然有效；精心设计的物理信息训练可在某些数据条件下匹敌或超越纯数据驱动方法。

Conclusion: 研究为PINO训练中的优化挑战提供了系统性实证理解，并给出了高效稳健的物理信息算子学习实践指南。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural operators (PINOs) aim to learn solution operators for partial differential equations by using the governing physics as supervision, rather than relying solely on paired input-output simulation data. By incorporating physical constraints into the training objective, PINOs combine the cross-instance generalization of neural operators with the data efficiency of physics-informed learning. Despite this promise, how to train PINOs efficiently and robustly remains less well-understood than the training of either data-driven neural operators or physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). To bridge this gap, we examine key components of the PINO training pipeline, including architecture design, optimizer choice, loss balancing, and collocation-point sampling strategy. We study three representative operator backbones, Deep Operator Network (DeepONet), Fourier Neural Operator (FNO), and Continuous Vision Transformer (CViT), across five diverse parametric PDE systems. Our results show that CViT provides consistently strong and stable performance across the considered benchmarks. Beyond architecture, we find that several optimization pathologies previously identified in PINN training naturally arise in PINOs, including gradient conflicts and causal violation. We also find that mitigation algorithms developed for PINNs remain effective in the PINO setting. We further compare physics-informed and data-driven training under different data regimes, revealing that a carefully designed physics-informed training pipeline can match, and in some cases, outperform purely data-driven neural operators. Taken together, these findings provide a systematic empirical understanding of the optimization challenges in PINO training and inform a practical pipeline for efficient and robust physics-informed operator learning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/NanxiiChen/PI-CViT.

</details>


### [76] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预算受限的微预训练场景中，采用分阶段分数阶因子实验工作流来识别稳定的早期效应结构。通过613次实验发现，总批次、深度和宽度在短预算时惩罚最大，随预算增加而放松；预声明种子全屏族中D、A、B、C在5/10分钟经BH校正后仍保留非零估计，而E不显著；随机搜索能找到强候选但缺乏因子归因；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但无法分离工作流优化与模型容量优势；在24小时三锚点延续中桥接锚点仍最低，非桥接排序对主机敏感。最终建议：用短设计屏幕识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有希望的锚点，在缩减空间内局部优化，支持在24小时内跨两台主机的桥接中心推荐，而非硬件不变排名或通用超参优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练中，需要在共享加速器上对大量候选配方进行筛选，然后才能投入更大的搜索预算。现有工作缺乏对这种分阶段分数阶因子工作流能否恢复稳定的早期效应结构进行系统研究。

Method: 在固定的自动研究衍生单GPU训练循环上，运行613次实验，涵盖2/5/10分钟的先导和跟踪屏幕、5/10分钟的完整16条件种子重跑、目标种子锚点检查、同机贪婪和匹配成本随机基线、60分钟桥接包，以及Windows A100和Linux L40S上24小时的有界锚点延续。

Result: 总批次、深度和宽度的主效应惩罚在短预算时最大，随预算增加而放松。在预声明种子全屏族中，D、A、B、C在5/10分钟经Benjamini-Hochberg校正后仍保留非零估计，E不显著。随机搜索可达到强候选但重复在低惩罚区域且无因子归因。60分钟桥接锚点均值最低但工作流与模型容量混淆。24小时延续中桥接锚点仍最低，非桥接排序主机敏感。

Conclusion: 提出有界方法学结果：用短设计屏幕识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有希望锚点，在缩减空间内局部优化。支持桥接中心推荐（24小时内跨两台主机），但不支持硬件不变排名或通用超参优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [77] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC，一个基于特征曲线（CC）骨架假设驱动方法的Python库，用于从时间序列数据中发现常微分方程（ODE），有效处理逆问题的不适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现是一个不适定逆问题，常产生多个拟合良好的候选模型。现有方法虽能通过先验假设缩小搜索空间，但仍需领域专家进行费力的手动后验筛选。

Method: 提出基于特征曲线（CC）的假设驱动方法论：用户定义与ODE族关联的"骨架"，添加领域知识假设和先验来迭代优化模型。该方法具有结构可辨识性，且模块化支持神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式。

Result: 开发了PyCC Python库，为研究人员和工程师提供灵活工具，可无缝定义骨架和假设，从时间依赖数据中发现ODE。

Conclusion: PyCC将基于CC骨架的假设驱动方程发现方法封装为易用的工具，解决了逆问题不适定性带来的多候选模型问题，并通过模块化设计支持多种发现范式。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [78] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法，在蒸馏LLM中找到了负责时间偏好的子图结构，发现LLM对未来折现比人类更平缓且偏好不稳定，并提供了通过引导向量调控时间偏好的初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景，但对其内部如何表征和解决这种权衡知之甚少，因此需要从机制可解释性角度理解LLM的时间偏好。

Method: 使用梯度归因和激活补丁的收敛证据，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507蒸馏模型中因果定位时间偏好的底层子图，识别出中间到上层节点；通过残差流几何分析时间跨度的编码；进行行为实验比较LLM与人类折现率差异。

Result: 时间偏好的子图位于中间到上层；残差流在预期层编码了时间跨度的几何结构；未干预的LLM对未来折现比人类平缓数倍，但这种偏好在不同上下文下不稳定；引导向量可以改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性有助于实现对LLM规划与推理中时间偏好的可靠控制，应追求显式控制而非隐式依赖训练。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [79] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习目标及反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），通过训练模型区分持久状态与临时计算，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著减少推理对隐藏思考的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型不区分用于计算的token和构成持久状态的token，导致后续推理可能依赖于错误的尝试、死胡同以及不应被安全依赖的私有草稿，这需要一种训练方法来显式区分临时计算与应提交的持久状态。

Method: 提出状态承诺学习训练目标，定义反事实准则（持久状态充分性）使其可训练和可测量；提出CERL，在相同前缀下评估保留隐藏思考与擦除隐藏思考两条路径，仅当擦除路径保持正确时给予奖励；同时引入擦除依赖协议。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学QA和多轮工具使用评估中，CERL大幅减少答案对隐藏思考的依赖，且不牺牲准确率，一致优于仅正确性RL和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: CERL能有效减少推理对隐藏思考的不当依赖，提升模型输出可靠性，同时保持下游任务性能。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [80] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度线性网络中，大步长离散梯度下降（GD）与梯度流（GF）在路径对称性破缺上的不同表现：GF预测“赢家通吃”的单路径特化，而大步长GD在边缘稳定性（Edge of Stability）阶段的振荡会推翻这种趋势，驱动信号在多路径间重新分配，从而促进共享表征而非单路径主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于梯度流（GF）的分析预测多路径深度线性网络中路径对称性破缺会导致“赢家通吃”的特化，但离散梯度下降（GD）在实际训练中更为常用，其动态行为（尤其是大步长下）与GF存在差异，需要从理论上解释这种差异及其对路径竞争的影响。

Method: 理论证明单路径解是尖锐极小值（sharp minima），而多路径分配信号可降低尖锐度，且降低因子随路径数和深度增加而减小；分析早期训练阶段（深度驱动的对称性破缺）与边缘稳定性（Edge of Stability）阶段的振荡行为，证明后者会覆盖前者并驱动信号再平衡。

Result: 早期训练重现了GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺，但边缘稳定性阶段的振荡会推翻这一趋势，导致信号在多路径间重新分布；大步长GD最终倾向于共享表征而非单路径持续主导。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了深度如何塑造路径竞争，并解释了大步长GD为何偏好共享表征而非单路径主导，揭示了离散优化与连续优化在隐式偏置上的本质差异。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [81] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分算子搜索框架EOS，将多种人工设计的token缩减算子统一到共享算子空间，并实现自动搜索最优的缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为，在多模态基准上取得竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基础模型依赖于人工设计的token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权），这些算子虽然形式各异但缺乏统一视角，且手动设计难以探索混合算子的全部潜力。

Method: 提出Efficient Operator Search (EOS)可微分框架，构建包含层激活、保留预算和算子行为的搜索空间，在单边预算和代价约束下联合优化任务性能，实现自动搜索缩减位置、保留数量和算子行为。

Result: 在多项多模态基准上，搜索得到的算子达到了竞争性的精度-效率权衡，尤其是在激进的视觉token缩减场景下表现优异。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可从手工算子设计转向可微分算子搜索，该框架能够恢复代表性人工基线并发现超越孤立手工设计的混合算子。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [82] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack提出了一种可微投影层，通过可学习松弛变量将非线性不等式约束转化为等式约束，并利用阻尼高斯-牛顿投影将网络输出映射到可行域内，实现了端到端可微的硬约束神经网络预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬约束方法在处理大量耦合的非线性不等式约束时，要么对约束集施加严格的结构限制，要么产生巨大的计算开销，缺乏可扩展的通用解决方案。

Method: 将不等式约束通过可学习松弛变量转化为等式约束，增强网络输出包含原始预测与松弛变量；采用阻尼高斯-牛顿投影将原始预测映射到增广可行流形上；设计两阶段课程学习策略稳定训练并提升约束满足度。

Result: 在车辆路径规划（200个非线性不等式约束）中，相比基线方法实现了更高规划成功率和更强几何约束满足度；消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性；CARLA闭环仿真和实车实验验证了轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack为将硬不等式约束嵌入神经网络提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，适用于工程应用。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [83] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTT-RTL，首个在测试时对每个RTL设计进行强化学习训练的框架，通过将LLM策略与EDA工具闭环集成，在生成功能正确代码的同时优化物理设计指标（PPA）。在RTLLM v2.0基准上PPA产品降低65.1%，远超冻结策略基线（26.1%），并在工业级XuanTie C910浮点单元上实现59.4%的ADP缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的RTL生成方法分为两类：训练时通过EDA反馈强化学习训练通用生成器，或测试时使用冻结策略搜索。前者无法针对具体设计问题自适应调整，后者策略固定、缺乏针对性优化。因此需要一种能在测试时根据具体RTL问题的EDA可执行反馈动态调整策略的方法。

Method: TTT-RTL框架包含四个核心组件：1）采样候选实现并通过语法检查和仿真验证；2）使用综合导出的PPA乘积对有效设计评分；3）通过PUCT索引的设计状态池重用高奖励变体；4）使用熵策略梯度目标更新策略。为稳定稀疏或平台期奖励下的策略更新，引入自适应KL预算控制器，根据参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号调节熵约束。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0基准（Nangate 45nm）上，TTT-RTL将几何平均PPA产品降低65.1%，优于最强冻结策略基线（26.1%）。在工业XuanTie C910 FPU前导零预测单元（Sky130）上实现59.4%的ADP缩减。消融实验验证了策略自适应、状态重用和KL预算控制三个组件的各自贡献。

Conclusion: 测试时训练与可执行EDA反馈的结合，能够将LLM驱动的RTL生成从单纯的功能正确性推进到物理优化的硬件设计阶段，为基于LLM的硬件设计自动化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [84] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flash-WAM，一种模态感知的步蒸馏框架，将世界-动作模型（WAM）的推理从数十步压缩到单步，在机器人操控任务上实现23倍加速（从8.1秒降至348毫秒），同时保持任务成功率（RoboTwin 2.0上85.5%，LIBERO上95.7%），并有效恢复真实世界性能（60%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）需要数十步去噪推理，成本过高无法实现实时控制。现有的步蒸馏方法无法处理视频和动作流使用不同SNR偏移噪声调度、训练时边际噪声分布显著不对称的问题。

Method: 提出Flash-WAM，基于一致性蒸馏的模态感知步蒸馏框架。通过结构分析一致性函数族，为动作流的低噪声区间选择线性梯度缩放参数化，为视频流的高噪声区间选择方差保持参数化，每个模态各压缩到单步推理。

Result: 在LingBot-VA上实例化，RoboTwin 2.0上每块延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速），实现实时推理；仿真成功率85.5%（RoboTwin 2.0）和95.7%（LIBERO），真实世界Unitree G1人形机器人平均60%；朴素一致性蒸馏在同样步数下仅24%。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM通过模态感知的蒸馏策略有效解决了多模态WAM的步蒸馏难题，在显著加速的同时保持任务性能，为实时机器人控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为基础模型（如推荐、支付、欺诈检测等领域的序列用户行为模型）的缩放定律。通过约600次实验，在10^15-10^19 FLOPs范围内，发现：小嵌入器（约2%参数）在计算最优性上占优；计算最优训练在低计算量时比文本模型更偏向数据密集型，但随着计算量增加趋近于Chinchilla启发式；训练目标与排序指标之间存在可缩放的偏差；评估指标本身会影响计算最优配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型已有成熟的缩放定律，但行为基础模型（基于用户行为序列训练的模型）缺乏类似的计算校准研究，无法指导计算资源的最优分配。

Method: 采用两部件行为模型架构（特征事件嵌入器+仅解码器Transformer），在真实交互数据上进行约600次实验，联合变化四轴：两部件参数分配、关键批量大小、模型/数据分配、冻结嵌入器后的负采样数量。

Result: 小嵌入器（约2%参数）在所有计算预算下计算最优；计算最优训练在低FLOPs时数据比重高于文本模型，随计算量增加趋近Chinchilla比率；训练目标与排序指标的偏差可缩放；更大预算更偏好更多负样本，到10^19 FLOPs时受候选轴内存限制。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标是缩放定律的一部分，改变指标可能改变计算最优配方。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [86] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CVT-RL算法，通过引入干预有效性门控、策略条件反事实贡献估计器和增强拉格朗日约束，解决长周期语言智能体在强化学习中的证据链断裂、信念漂移和捷径行为问题，在长上下文QA、ALFWorld等任务中将平均成功率提升至78.9%，证据F1提升至82.8，黑客行为降至3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法虽然改进了推理和工具使用能力，但长周期语言智能体仍存在学习无支撑证据链、信念漂移和仅满足终端检查的捷径动作问题。现有的过程奖励大多是相关的，奖励检索、反思或验证类步骤，但未估计这些步骤在特定干预下是否对最终验证成功有贡献。

Method: 提出CVT-RL算法，包含：(1) 密集可验证奖励和干预有效性门控；(2) 策略条件反事实贡献(PCCC)估计器，通过删除、语义替换、证据替换和工具输出扰动四种受控干预，从冻结参考策略采样continuation，并使用选择调整的双重稳健估计器增强优势；(3) 信念控制仅使用前缀可观测标签；(4) 增强拉格朗日约束限制无支撑声明、跳过验证、工具篡改和不安全调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和web/tool任务上，CVT-RL将平均任务成功率从计算匹配的非因果RL的71.8%和信息匹配的反事实过程基线的75.4%提升至78.9%，证据F1从78.9提升至82.8（对比信息匹配基线），测量的黑客行为从7.2%降至3.9%。独立人工审计估计CVT-RL黑客行为为4.6%（对比基线的8.1%），自适应检测器规避攻击仅将黑客行为提升至7.1%。分层bootstrap和混合效应检验对所有主要指标给出p<0.01（Holm校正后）。

Conclusion: 精心设计的反事实信用分配与有效性门控、诊断和可验证约束相结合，为更可靠的长周期语言智能体强化学习提供了一条可复现的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [87] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGeN是一种参考引导的生成框架，将观测序列分解为周期主干、随机残差和跨变量依赖三个可解释组件，通过可控采样生成高质量多元时间序列数据，在低数据场景下比现有方法更有效地替代真实数据，甚至在某些领域超越真实数据效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中多元时间序列数据匮乏，现有生成方法（基于先验或数据驱动）要么生成领域无关的样本，要么缺乏对周期结构、局部变异和跨变量动态的显式控制，无法解决数据稀缺与模型训练需求之间的不匹配问题。

Method: ReGeN将观测序列作为结构支架而非模仿对象，分解为三个组件：(1) 相位对齐的周期主干（捕获领域形态）；(2) 每变量随机残差（深度核高斯过程建模）；(3) 滞后感知的跨变量依赖（结构因果模型+拟合耦合系数）。通过可控温度采样扩展分布覆盖范围。

Result: ReGeN生成的数据能一致地替代真实兄弟数据且预测性能下降极小；在强周期领域（如交通）甚至优于真实数据；基于ReGeN语料库预训练的基座模型优于基于先验和数据驱动合成数据训练的结果。

Conclusion: 在低数据场景下，如何结构化利用参考数据与拥有多少数据同样重要。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [88] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种领域感知的核心集构建流程，通过仅使用每个流域0.7%的训练数据，在推理时条件化表格基础模型，实现了跨流域可迁移的洪水深度预测，平均R²达到0.663，达到监督参考模型98.5%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近实时洪水深度预测需要精确、快速且跨流域可迁移的代理模型。监督式代理模型虽能匹配物理模拟器的精度，但每个流域需要数百万训练样本，且无法外推到原始网格之外。

Method: 提出领域感知的核心集构建流程：(1) 按重现期和受影响最严重的流域对风暴进行分层；(2) 使用目标感知的空间选择器对六边形进行采样；(3) 在推理时条件化表格基础模型。

Result: 仅用每个流域0.7%的训练数据，在休斯顿地区9个流域上平均R²达0.663，达到监督参考（R²=0.673）的98.5%。无需特定任务重训练即可迁移到未见的流域。在远分布外真实风暴上超越监督参考，在近分布内案例上略逊。

Conclusion: 领域感知的核心集构建使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、跨流域可迁移的洪水预测，无需每个流域单独训练。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [89] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在planted-vs-planted设定下，为低度多项式检验建立了首个尖锐阈值，证明了planted submatrix和planted dense subgraph模型中计数社区的检验阈值与恢复阈值精确匹配，并识别了弱检验的平滑转变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在planted-vs-planted设定中，目标是以渐近零错误区分两个结构化机制产生的数据，但此前缺乏低度多项式检验的精确阈值分析。

Method: 基于低度恢复中的潜变量展开框架，发展新的方法来识别和剪除非信号贡献。

Result: 获得了匹配的尖锐上下界，检验阈值与已知低度恢复阈值精确一致；弱检验呈现平滑转变而非尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 揭示了planted-vs-planted检验与恢复任务之间的深层联系，并为该领域提供了首个精确阈值分析框架。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [90] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），通过Hopf代数框架将NRDE的对数ODE步骤重构为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分，匹配驱动代数与对应微积分（Grossman-Larson树用于欧几里得Itô动力学，Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面树用于流形上有序协变导数，shuffle代数用于经典Stratonovich情形），并引入分支签名核目标使二次变差项在训练中可见，从而在非均匀采样和粗步长下实现精确的Itô动力学和流形约束保持，在粗糙Bergomi波动率、sim-to-real SO(3)动力学和SPD协方差动力学任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经粗糙微分方程（NRDEs）在非均匀采样下精度高、积分步数少，但其基于shuffle代数（Stratonovich演算的代数对应）的设计使其无法表达Itô动力学所需的二次变差项，也无法处理带联络流形上Itô流所需的有序协变导数。本文旨在突破这一局限，建立能统一处理Stratonovich、Itô以及流形上微分方程的神经粗糙微分方程框架。

Method: 提出B-NRDEs框架：将NRDE的对数ODE步骤重构为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分，根据所处理的动力学类型选择对应的Hopf代数结构（Grossman-Larson树、Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面树或shuffle代数），从而匹配驱动代数与目标微积分；并引入分支签名核目标函数，使二次变差项在训练阶段可见，实现Itô一致的法律匹配。

Result: 在三个典型任务上验证有效性：粗糙Bergomi波动率建模（Itô随机过程）、sim-to-real SO(3)动力学预测（流形上的有序协变导数）、SPD协方差动力学（流形约束保持）。B-NRDEs在这些超越欧几里得-Stratonovich设定的任务中展现出统一且有效的能力。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs通过Hopf代数框架将NRDE推广到更一般的随机和流形值动力学场景，提供了超越传统欧几里得-Stratonovich设定的统一方法论，兼顾了理论优雅性与实际有效性。
